# <b>{ "176" } of { "388" }</b> capabilities validated against independent external oracles; { "208" } honestly labelled Modelled, { "4" } partner-owned. Each capability carries one label in a machine-checked ledger: VALIDATED (an independent external oracle agrees: real data, an independent implementation or published reference vectors), MODELLED (internally consistent, and said out loud) or PARTNER (a hardware partner owns it). Among the checks: all { "666" } SGP4 (Simplified General Perturbations { "4" }) vectors of AIAA (American Institute of Aeronautics and Astronautics) 2006-6753 to { "4.12" } mm, and the Cowell force model to { "0.08" } m against Orekit { "12.2" }.

# Kshana Studio, Simple view, on the bundled scenario integrity-raim, engine { "v0.38.0" } running locally. The question: does the geometry meet the alert limits (horizontal and vertical protection level)? The answer first, marked Live result: Yes, mostly: for { "95.3" } % of the { "12" } h. The receiver's error bound stayed under the alert limits ({ "40" } m horizontal and { "50" } m vertical) at { "344" } of { "361" } checks, and no fix was misleading. Key figures: time the fix can be trusted { "95.3" } %, longest gap { "10" } min, typical error bound { "6.5" } / { "13" } m, misleading fixes { "0" }. Beside it, Try other settings with five of them (the range error, the two alert limits, the duration and the elevation mask) and Run again, then the folded sections Advanced settings ({ "13" } more), How this is computed ({ "8" } validated, { "6" } modelled) and For researchers. Below, the views of this result and the protection levels plotted against the alert limit.

# How a run flows. A scenario TOML file (a kind, a seed and its parameters) goes into the engine, kshana { $version }, through the api::run_toml dispatch over { "87" } scenario kinds, deterministic from scenario, seed and engine version. The engine writes result.json, chart.svg, report.html and report.json, a table.csv for the kinds that define one, and on request SP3, CCSDS OMM and OEM, CZML, KML, GeoJSON, STK and SigMF exports; a suite writes study.json and study.html. Those files feed Kshana Studio in the browser, an AI assistant through the kshana-mcp server, and continuous integration.
seg-76e3ec458441 = 一次运行的流程。一个场景TOML文件（类型、随机种子及其参数）进入引擎kshana { $version }，经由api::run_toml在{ "87" }种场景类型上分派，由场景、随机种子和引擎版本确定性地决定。引擎写出result.json、chart.svg、report.html和report.json，为定义了表格的类型写出table.csv，并按需写出SP3、CCSDS OMM和OEM、CZML、KML、GeoJSON、STK和SigMF导出文件；一个套件会写出study.json和study.html。这些文件供浏览器中的Kshana Studio、通过kshana-mcp服务器接入的AI助手以及持续集成使用。

# <b>{ "176" } of { "388" }</b> capabilities validated against independent external oracles; { "208" } honestly labelled Modelled. Each row of the verification matrix names a capability, the oracle it is checked against, the test that runs the check, and the label that follows. Continuous integration (CI) makes it impossible to call a capability VALIDATED without an independent external oracle.
seg-707e2cf795f6 = <b>{ "388" }项能力中有{ "176" }项</b>已对照独立的外部参考基准（oracle）检验；{ "208" }项如实标注为由模型计算得出。验证矩阵的每一行都写明一项能力、用来核对它的参考基准、运行该核对的测试，以及随之得出的标签。持续集成（CI）使得没有独立的外部参考基准就无法把某项能力称为VALIDATED。

# How a capability earns its label. Each row of the verification matrix names a capability, the oracle it is checked against, the test that runs the check in continuous integration, and the label that follows. A row may be VALIDATED only with an independent external oracle; otherwise it is MODELLED, or PARTNER when a hardware partner owns it. Example: SGP4/SDP4, Cowell 6-DOF + perturbations, batch/sequential OD, oracle AIAA 2006-6753 SGP4 verification vectors, test tests/sgp4_verification.rs, label VALIDATED. Live counts: { "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER of { "388" } rows. In all, { "176" } capabilities validated against independent external oracles; { "208" } more are honestly labelled MODELLED and { "4" } are PARTNER-owned.
seg-2f81cf391eae = 一项能力如何获得其标签。验证矩阵的每一行都写明一项能力、用来核对它的参考基准、在持续集成中运行该核对的测试，以及随之得出的标签。只有具备独立的外部参考基准时，一行才可以是VALIDATED；否则为MODELLED，而由硬件合作方负责的则为PARTNER。示例：SGP4/SDP4、Cowell 6-DOF + 摄动、批处理/序贯定轨，参考基准为AIAA 2006-6753 SGP4验证向量，测试为tests/sgp4_verification.rs，标签为VALIDATED。实时计数：{ "388" }行中{ "176" }行VALIDATED，{ "208" }行MODELLED，{ "4" }行PARTNER。总计{ "176" }项能力已对照独立的外部参考基准检验；另有{ "208" }项如实标注为MODELLED，{ "4" }项由PARTNER负责。

seg-ed47a99fac7f = 部分核对项：全部{ "666" }个AIAA（美国航空航天学会）2006-6753 SGP4（Simplified General Perturbations { "4" }）向量，吻合至{ "4.12" } mm；Cowell力模型对照Orekit { "12.2" }，吻合至{ "0.08" } m；Galileo吻合至{ "0.61" } m，Swarm-A吻合至{ "0.10" } m，均对照欧洲空间局（ESA）的真实精密星历。测试使<c1/>的行覆盖率接近{ "95" } %，在CI中以{ "85" } % 为门限。台账为<a1><c2/></a1>，由<c3/>生成：<a2>完整的388行矩阵</a2>，以及每一行由模型计算得出的结果为何没有外部参考基准，见<a3><c4/></a3>。

# Every row is enforced by a named test in CI. This table is a <b>curated highlight</b>; the full machine-checked matrix is <b>{ "388" } rows — { "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER</b> (<c1/>), with the complete evidence (and what is honestly <em>not</em> yet validated) in <a1><c2/></a1> and the per-release <a2><c3/></a2> artifact (generated by <c4/>, SLSA-attested — Supply-chain Levels for Software Artifacts).

# How a capability earns its label: Requirement maps to a module in src, to a test in tests, to an external oracle (real dataset, independent reference implementation, or published vectors), to a status — with a CI-enforced guard that no capability can be Validated without an external oracle. Live counts: { "176" } Validated, { "208" } Modelled, { "4" } Partner, { "388" } total
seg-9fcd5cdd129d = 一项能力如何获得其标签：需求对应src中的某个模块，对应tests中的某项测试，对应某个外部参考基准（oracle，即真实数据集、独立的参考实现或已发布的测试向量），再对应一个状态；并由CI强制执行一道防护：没有外部参考基准的能力不能被标为已核对。实时计数：已核对{ "176" }项，已建模{ "208" }项，合作方负责{ "4" }项，共{ "388" }项

# Kshana's architecture: one open engine at the centre, kshana { $version } under the AGPL-3.0, with api::run_toml, a typed dispatch over every kind, and the verification ledger of { "388" } capabilities ({ "176" } VALIDATED, { "208" } MODELLED, { "4" } PARTNER). Around it, every surface runs the same engine: Command line (cargo install kshana); Rust library (cargo add kshana); Python (pip install kshana); WebAssembly + Kshana Studio (npm install kshana); MCP server (cargo install kshana-mcp); Docker image (ghcr.io/ashfordeou/kshana-mcp); JetBrains plugin (Marketplace: "Kshana"). Below it, Kshana Pro, a proprietary overlay that depends on the open engine as a library and never forks it, and adds no physical model.
seg-9d69cf43cd40 = Kshana的架构：中心是一个开放的引擎，即AGPL-3.0下的kshana { $version }，带有api::run_toml、对每种类型的类型化分派，以及含{ "388" }项能力的验证台账（{ "176" }项VALIDATED，{ "208" }项MODELLED，{ "4" }项PARTNER）。围绕它，每个界面运行同一个引擎：命令行（cargo install kshana）；Rust库（cargo add kshana）；Python（pip install kshana）；WebAssembly + Kshana Studio（npm install kshana）；MCP服务器（cargo install kshana-mcp）；Docker镜像（ghcr.io/ashfordeou/kshana-mcp）；JetBrains插件（Marketplace：“Kshana”）。其下方是Kshana Pro，一个专有叠加层，以库的形式依赖开放的引擎，绝不分叉它，也不增加任何物理模型。

# Kshana's architecture in layers. Five front doors (command line, Python, JavaScript and WebAssembly, the MCP server and the JetBrains plugin) reach one api::run_toml, a typed dispatch over { "87" } kinds. Beneath it sit eight domain layers: time and frames, clocks and timing, inertial and fusion, GNSS and integrity, astrodynamics, LEO PNT, Moon, Mars and deep space, and missions and studies, on a shared core, all cross-referenced by the verification module, the machine-checked matrix of { "388" } capabilities that is the single source of truth for every label.
seg-9bc9a971bdb4 = Kshana的分层架构。五个入口（命令行、Python、JavaScript与WebAssembly、MCP服务器和JetBrains插件）都通向同一个api::run_toml，即对{ "87" }种类型的类型化分派。其下是八个领域层：时间与坐标系、时钟与授时、惯性与融合、GNSS与完好性、天体动力学、LEO PNT、月球、火星与深空，以及任务与研究，建立在共享的核心之上，全部由验证模块交叉引用；该模块是对{ "388" }项能力经机器检查的矩阵，是每个标签的唯一事实来源。

# <b>{ "176" } of { "388" }</b> capabilities validated against independent external oracles; { "208" } honestly labelled Modelled, { "4" } partner-owned. Each capability carries one label in a machine-checked ledger: VALIDATED (an independent external oracle agrees: real data, an independent implementation or published reference vectors), MODELLED (internally consistent, and said out loud) or PARTNER (a hardware partner owns it). Among the checks: all { "666" } SGP4 (Simplified General Perturbations { "4" }) vectors of AIAA (American Institute of Aeronautics and Astronautics) 2006-6753 to { "4.12" } mm, and the Cowell force model to { "0.08" } m against Orekit { "12.2" }.
seg-2d2c47db0c1f = <b>{ "388" }项能力中有{ "176" }项</b>已对照独立的外部基准（oracle）核对；{ "208" }项如实标为由模型计算得出，{ "4" }项由合作方负责。每项能力在经机器检查的台账（ledger）中都带有一个标签：VALIDATED（独立的外部基准（oracle）一致：真实数据、独立实现或已发布的参考向量）、MODELLED（内部自洽，并明确说明）或PARTNER（由硬件合作方负责）。检查包括：AIAA（美国航空航天学会）2006-6753的全部{ "666" }个SGP4（简化通用摄动{ "4" }）向量，精度达{ "4.12" } mm，以及对照Orekit { "12.2" }的Cowell力模型，精度达{ "0.08" } m。

# How a capability earns its label: capability, oracle, test, ledger label. The verification status across all { "388" } capabilities: { "176" } Validated, { "208" } Modelled, { "4" } Partner-owned

# Three independent tools, pre-registered (0123cee1) before any fixture or oracle output existed, tolerances fixed then. ({ "1" }) ARAIM: Stanford MAAST for ARAIM { "2" } (commit ab70e2a3, BSD-3) mhss_raim_baseline_v5.m, run as a separate program under GNU Octave { "8.4" } with a driver-side unique() compatibility shim (disclosed, no algorithm change), at the ADD TOL_PL of { "0.05" } m: the Rust reproduces the ADD reference implementation on { "270" } real cases. Disclosed: the Kshana ADD path was written after the pre-registration, following the ADD as MAAST implements it; an independent hold-out by the reviewer ({ "270" } new user geometries and a second integrity support message, MAAST re-run against the frozen code) passed at the same bar. ({ "2" }) Snapshot RAIM: RTKLIB v2.4.2-p13 (BSD-2) estpos/valsol/raim_fde in a separate C harness for detection and exclusion; the slope PL from RTKLIB matinv/xyz2enu plus SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2, where both sides evaluate the same closed form (Brown; Parkinson and Axelrad), so the PL check covers the implementation, not the formula. ({ "3" }) SBAS: Stanford MAAST (commit 7d32b049) usr_vhpl, unmodified, on MAAST own recorded WAAS broadcasts: L1 levels within { "1e-4" } m after the K rescaling and an equal protected set. The same comparisons produced the findings stated in the capability: the uniform-sigma ARAIM functions are not ADD-conformant, and the L5 set equality fails on { "308" } pairs with no GEO in view (the levels on the { "3267" } pairs both tools protect agree to { "1e-13" } m). The DO-229E/DO-316 K-factors are transcribed constants and the real geometry is an input; neither is counted as an oracle. { "0.30" } revision: snapshot_raim protection levels moved from the radial to the geodetic local level (up to { "0.31" } m HPL and { "0.14" } m VPL at { "16" } deg N)
seg-a83c68500982 = 三个独立工具，均在任何测试夹具或参考基准输出出现之前预先登记（0123cee1），容差在当时固定。（{ "1" }）ARAIM：Stanford MAAST for ARAIM { "2" }（提交ab70e2a3，BSD-3）mhss_raim_baseline_v5.m，在GNU Octave { "8.4" }下作为独立程序运行，驱动端带有unique() 兼容性垫片（已披露，不改变算法），采用ADD的TOL_PL { "0.05" } m：该Rust实现在{ "270" }个真实算例上复现了ADD参考实现。已披露：Kshana的ADD路径是在预先登记之后、按照MAAST实现ADD的方式编写的；审阅者另做了独立保留集检验（{ "270" }个新的用户几何以及第二条完好性支持电文，针对冻结代码重新运行MAAST），在同一判据下通过。（{ "2" }）快照RAIM：RTKLIB v2.4.2-p13（BSD-2）的estpos/valsol/raim_fde，在单独的C测试程序中用于检测与剔除；斜率PL取自RTKLIB matinv/xyz2enu加SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2，两侧计算的是同一个闭式表达式（Brown；Parkinson与Axelrad），因此PL检查覆盖的是实现，而不是公式。（{ "3" }）SBAS：Stanford MAAST（提交7d32b049）的usr_vhpl，未经修改，用于MAAST自己记录的WAAS广播：L1保护级在K重新缩放之后相差在{ "1e-4" } m以内，且受保护集相等。同样的比较得出了能力描述中所述的各项发现：统一sigma的ARAIM函数不符合ADD，并且L5集合相等性在{ "308" }个视野内没有GEO的历元对上不成立（两个工具都提供保护的{ "3267" }个历元对上，其保护级吻合至{ "1e-13" } m）。DO-229E/DO-316的K因子是抄录的常数，真实几何是输入；两者都不算作参考基准。{ "0.30" }版修订：snapshot_raim的保护级由径向改为大地测量局部水平面（在{ "16" }度N处，HPL最多变化{ "0.31" } m，VPL最多变化{ "0.14" } m）

# GMAT R2022a (NASA GSFC, Apache-2.0) GmatConsole with the Exponential atmosphere (the same 28-band table), point-mass Earth and PrinceDormand78, run, never vendored. Bars pre-registered in xval/drag-exponential-gmat/PREREGISTRATION.md (640c85c1) on the drag displacement of each side (drag minus no-drag run), which cancels the two Earth mu values and integrators; all met (RESULTS.md). GMAT takes geodetic height and kshana spherical altitude: they coincide only on the equator, so VALIDATED covers equatorial orbits; off the equator the models differ by design ({ "10.4" } % of { "24" } h displacement at { "51.6" } deg, { "400" } km). FINDING on the 18-event bar campaign: (c) PASS; (a') NOT MET, delta_eff error -1.097 dB against { "0.5" } dB (still -0.89 dB on bins at { "38" } dB-Hz and above); (b') NOT MET, { "0" } of { "50" } campaigns cover the truth (error -1.189 +/- { "0.093" } dB, interval about { "0.3" } dB); (d) NOT MET, closed form { "2.20" } dB below the direct J/S at loss, about the fitted drop dwell times the ramp rate ({ "2.09" } dB); the original Q clauses of (a) and (b) are NOT MET because Q is not identifiable. FOLLOW-UP (bars pre-registered in 1e532f84, docs/design/evidence/labfit-followup/RESULTS.md): on a stepped-J/S campaign delta_eff is recovered within +{ "0.185" } dB (S1 MET: the ramp error is estimator lag); (d) as written on it is met by the tracking-loop model ({ "0.45" } dB) but not the empirical one ({ "6.42" } dB), so S2 is NOT MET; on a fresh ramp campaign (d) with the fitted drop dwell times each event's ramp rate added is within { "1" } dB for both models (R1 MET), and (a') and (d) as written miss again (-1.25 dB; { "2.32" } and { "9.47" } dB). The method is checked, not any receiver: it cannot be VALIDATED from synthetic data
seg-f9abecb1eea6 = 在Kshana自己的GPS L1 C/A场景上做合成参数恢复，场景中施加按第{ "3.1" }节公式得到的已知C/N0剖面，由kshana iq campaign跟踪并评分，判据在docs/design/LABFIT-FROM-IQ.md（第{ "5.1" }节，2026-10-12在b7117bf中修订，第{ "13" }节）中预先登记。测评活动的C/N0估计器（M2M4）在5b13e3c2中固定，在首次判据评估之前推送（与拟合代码一同提交，而非单独提交），此前有一次已披露的诊断性跟踪运行。对18事件判据测评活动的发现：（c）PASS；（a'）NOT MET，delta_eff误差为-1.097 dB，对比{ "0.5" } dB（在{ "38" } dB-Hz及以上的分箱上仍为-0.89 dB）；（b'）NOT MET，{ "50" }个测评活动中有{ "0" }个覆盖真值（误差-1.189 +/- { "0.093" } dB，区间约{ "0.3" } dB）；（d）NOT MET，闭式值在失锁时比直接J/S低{ "2.20" } dB，约等于拟合的失锁驻留时间乘以斜坡速率（{ "2.09" } dB）；（a）和（b）原有的Q条款为NOT MET，因为Q不可辨识。后续（判据在1e532f84中预先登记，docs/design/evidence/labfit-followup/RESULTS.md）：在阶跃J/S测评活动上，delta_eff恢复到+{ "0.185" } dB以内（S1 MET：斜坡误差是估计器滞后）；（d）按所写在其上由跟踪环路模型达到判据（{ "0.45" } dB），经验模型则未达到（{ "6.42" } dB），因此S2为NOT MET；在新的斜坡测评活动上，用拟合的失锁驻留时间加上每个事件的斜坡速率，（d）对两种模型均在{ "1" } dB以内（R1 MET），而（a'）和（d）按所写再次未达到（-1.25 dB；{ "2.32" }和{ "9.47" } dB）。被检验的是方法，而不是任何接收机：它不能从合成数据得到VALIDATED

Coverage is { "0.00" } on both circles against >= { "0.95" }, and the corrected p95 3-D error is { "280.6" } m ({ "300" } km), and { "293.0" } m at { "600" } s latency, against <= { "1.5" } m. The decimetre bar (K4) could not pass at the sources' { "0.30" } m code noise, which reflects a conflict between the sources rather than a model result. K6 passes (ratio { "1.39" } against <= { "1.5" }), but as a ratio of two geometry-dominated figures it is evidence about decorrelation in neither direction. Cause: the satellites' elements are not public, and on the ILLUSTRATIVE in-tree elements a user and the station share four satellites above { "5" } deg in only { "8.2" } % ({ "300" } km) and { "6.3" } % ({ "600" } km) of epochs, at near-singular geometry (uncorrected p95 { "3.8" } km against the sources' < { "10" } m). The sources' simulations used a filter, a height constraint and three satellites in common view, which the snapshot kind does not model. The comparison therefore neither confirms nor refutes the published 'sub-metre over the whole south pole'. It shows that this model's result depends on orbits that are not published. Not a statement about any real system; not affiliated with or endorsed by any agency

({ "2" }) The textbook closed-form least-squares solution, different algebra from the matrix solve under test, on real rows. ({ "3" }) The genuine archived Bulletin A prediction rows the real { "2026" } product publishes, compared per lead as an AGREEMENT statistic and explicitly not as an error: { "0.256" } ms at { "1" } day, { "0.695" } ms at { "2" } days, { "1.252" } ms at { "3" } days, drifting to { "3.885" } ms at { "9" } days. So this is Bulletin A's CLASS, close at short lead, not Bulletin A. The predicted-versus-final residuals are real measured quantities over real IERS rows, but their magnitude is checked only against the DIRECTION of the comparison, never against an IERS-published accuracy number — reading a real product is provenance, not an oracle. # Closed-form identities and an independent in-repo route, with no external oracle claimed. The Helmert design — the only new derivative in the module — is re-derived by central finite difference of lunar_frame_realise::apply_helmert, a path sharing no expression with the analytic form. The accumulation it composes is lunar-vlbi-fim's, whose Jacobian is finite-differenced against lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s there, and whose linear-algebra kernel is separately externally anchored against numpy in tests/fim_observability_reference.rs — this row borrows neither status. The physics oracle is structural rather than numerical: a beacon delay partial is the near-field DIFFERENCE of two near-parallel unit vectors, so the worst-determined translation direction MUST be the body-fixed direction to Earth, and the test asserts the computed answer against the separately measured direction. MEASURED: campaign-derived translation sigma { "6.3975" } m against the injected-transform scenario's { "0.3125" } m recovery error ({ "20.5" }× tighter), while rotation and scale run the OTHER way at { "2.32" }× and { "13.5" }× looser — the injected path assumes one isotropic sigma and so spreads its error uniformly across seven parameters, which the delay observable does not. Rank 7/7 but condition { "3.8e5" }, with one direction { "99.57" } % pure translation-toward-Earth and forty times worse than any other. WHAT THIS ROW DOES NOT CLAIM: that the campaign figure is right in absolute terms. It establishes that the figure now DERIVES from a schedule, a geometry and an error model rather than from a planted answer. Stays ReferenceImpl/MODELLED: no published lunar-VLBI campaign-plus-datum-covariance pair exists, the station network and delay sigma are ILLUSTRATIVE inputs (the beacon catalogue is sourced, the campaign is not), observations are treated as independent while a real session's troposphere and clock are correlated between nearby scans, and the ephemeris, clocks, troposphere and Earth-orientation parameters are held FIXED. { "0.30" } external comparison, a finding (stays MODELLED): with every beacon Jacobian row rebuilt from NAIF SPICE light times (DE440, ITRF93, MOON_ME; pre-registered { "50863551" }), the schedule, Helmert rank, datum sigmas (within { "4.7e-3" }), condition, weakest direction ({ "0.07" } deg), libration sweep and arc ordering agree on six scenarios within the pre-registered { "1" } % / { "0.2" } / { "0.05" } deg. NumPy on the committed engine Jacobian agrees to { "3e-14" } where the stations are fixed. With the stations estimated it differs by { "6.8e-6" } against a pre-registered { "1e-9" }, whose condition-number premise was wrong (Helmert condition { "2.1e8" }; independent NumPy routes disagree by { "3e-6" }; a contribution from the engine { "1e-9" } pseudo-inverse fallback is not ruled out), so the row is not promoted on that pre-registration. { "0.36" } re-registration at a condition-scaled bar (pre-registered b887eb05; t = max({ "1e-12" }, { "10" } kappa { "2" }^-53) from the conditioning of the joint information and its Helmert reduction), a finding (stays MODELLED, NOT MET): step { "0" } rules the pseudo-inverse fallback OUT for the { "6.8e-6" } gap (no matrix upstream of the Helmert matrix falls back on the six original scenarios, tightest margin a factor { "2.6" }), the SPICE leg still holds on all six, and NumPy agrees within the bar on six of eight scenarios (default, the two arcs, the 3x delay sigma, the collinear defect and the fresh stations-fixed run on 2026-12-01; worst { "0.13" } of its bar). It fails on stations_estimated (datum sigmas up to { "6.8e-6" }, condition { "9.1e-6" }, independence-discarded norm { "3.5e-6" }, against t = { "5.9e-7" }), and on the fresh date the south-pole beacon has no observation, so the beacon information is rank-deficient (pseudo-inverse fallback upstream of the Helmert matrix in both fresh scenarios) and the unchanged NumPy oracle cannot evaluate stations_estimated_fresh (singular matrix). Results: docs/design/evidence/mr05-lunar-datum-cond/RESULTS.md
seg-8eedf0240350 = 闭式恒等式和一条仓库内独立的路线，不声称有任何外部参考基准。Helmert设计矩阵是该模块中唯一新增的导数，它通过对lunar_frame_realise::apply_helmert做中心有限差分重新推导，这条路径与解析形式不共用任何表达式。它所组合的累积来自lunar-vlbi-fim，其雅可比矩阵在那里对lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s做有限差分，其线性代数内核又在tests/fim_observability_reference.rs中单独对numpy做了外部锚定；本行不借用这两者的地位。物理参考基准是结构性的，而不是数值性的：信标延迟偏导数是两个近乎平行的单位矢量的近场之差，所以确定得最差的平移方向必然是指向地球的体固方向，而测试把计算出的答案与另行MEASURED的方向对照断言。实测：由观测活动得到的平移sigma为{ "6.3975" } m，而注入变换场景的恢复误差为{ "0.3125" } m（紧{ "20.5" }×），同时旋转和尺度则朝相反方向，分别松{ "2.32" }× 和{ "13.5" }×；注入路径假设单一的各向同性sigma，因此把误差均匀分摊到七个参数上，而延迟观测量并非如此。秩7/7，但条件数为{ "3.8e5" }，其中一个方向有{ "99.57" } % 是纯粹的指向地球的平移，且比任何其他方向差四十倍。本行不声称的内容：观测活动的数字在绝对意义上是正确的。它确立的只是，该数字现在由计划、几何和误差模型推导而来，而不是来自预先植入的答案。仍为ReferenceImpl/MODELLED：不存在已发表的月面VLBI观测活动加基准协方差的配对，测站网络和延迟sigma是ILLUSTRATIVE输入（信标目录有来源，观测活动没有），观测被按独立处理，而真实观测时段中邻近扫描之间的对流层和时钟是相关的，星历、时钟、对流层和地球定向参数则保持固定。{ "0.30" }外部比较，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：每一行信标雅可比矩阵都用NAIF SPICE光行时重建（DE440、ITRF93、MOON_ME；预先登记{ "50863551" }），计划、Helmert秩、基准sigma（在{ "4.7e-3" }以内）、条件数、最弱方向（{ "0.07" }度）、天平动扫描和弧长排序在六个场景上一致，处于预先登记的{ "1" } % / { "0.2" } / { "0.05" }度以内。在测站固定的情形下，NumPy对已提交的引擎雅可比矩阵的一致性达到{ "3e-14" }。测站被估计时，差异为{ "6.8e-6" }，而预先登记的是{ "1e-9" }，其条件数的前提是错误的（Helmert条件数为{ "2.1e8" }；独立的NumPy路线之间相差{ "3e-6" }；不能排除引擎{ "1e-9" }伪逆回退的贡献），因此本行不据该预先登记升级。{ "0.36" }以随条件数缩放的判据重新登记（预先登记b887eb05；t = max({ "1e-12" }, { "10" } kappa { "2" }^-53)，由联合信息矩阵及其Helmert约化的条件数得出），一项发现（仍为MODELLED，NOT MET）：第{ "0" }步排除了伪逆回退是造成{ "6.8e-6" }差距的原因（在原先六个场景中，Helmert矩阵上游没有任何矩阵发生回退，最紧的裕量为{ "2.6" }倍），SPICE环节在全部六个场景上仍成立，NumPy在八个场景中的六个在判据内一致（默认、两种弧长、3x延迟sigma、共线亏秩，以及2026-12-01的全新测站固定运行；最差为其判据的{ "0.13" }）。它在stations_estimated上失败（基准sigma最高{ "6.8e-6" }，条件数{ "9.1e-6" }，舍弃独立性后的范数{ "3.5e-6" }，而t = { "5.9e-7" }），并且在全新日期上，南极信标没有任何观测，因此信标信息秩亏（两个全新场景中Helmert矩阵上游都出现伪逆回退），未更改的NumPy参考基准无法评估stations_estimated_fresh（矩阵奇异）。结果：docs/design/evidence/mr05-lunar-datum-cond/RESULTS.md

NO lunar-navigation constellation kernel exists publicly — Moonlight/LCNS, LCRNS and LNSS are not flying and NAIF publishes nothing for them — and none was invented. The DERIVED σ_URE requirement has NO external oracle (nobody publishes the ranging accuracy a { "50" } m lunar HPL demands over this service volume), so it is checked against its own algebraic identity and, independently, by re-running the whole sweep at the computed requirement and confirming availability flips there. The only new derivative — the range partial with respect to the reflector's body-fixed position — is re-derived by central finite difference of the two-way light time it differentiates, a path sharing no expression with the analytic form. # RTKLIB v2.4.2-p13 (T. # Closed-form identities and internal consistency; NO external oracle is claimed. The limb half angle is checked against asin(R_earth/r_orbit) and the geometry against it; the free-space loss, the DOP kernel and the SGP4 propagation this composes are each externally validated in their own rows and this row does not borrow their status. WHY MODELLED, and it is the transmit pattern: the gain at angle is a uniformly illuminated circular aperture, the Airy pattern, while a real GPS L1 antenna is a twelve-element helical array with a shaped main lobe peaked off-boresight to even out power across the Earth disc and sidelobes that are not Airy. Published measured patterns exist and are not vendored here, so orderings and orders of magnitude carry, while the dB of any single satellite does not. A FIRST IMPLEMENTATION OF THIS ROW WAS WRONG IN A WAY WORTH RECORDING: the Airy expression is valid only in the forward hemisphere, its argument goes as sin(theta), and at theta near { "180" } deg it wraps around and returns FULL BORESIGHT GAIN behind the aperture - so the two strongest links in the first report were satellites pointing their antennas away from the Moon, and they were the only two that cleared the threshold. Behind the aperture the model now applies a flat back-lobe floor, stated as a bound rather than a fabricated pattern shape. Deliberately absent, each making the budget OPTIMISTIC: no ionospheric or tropospheric loss on the limb-grazing rays, no polarisation, pointing or implementation loss, and a spherical Earth with no refractive extension. The Moon position is an INPUT, not an ephemeris lookup, because the quantity under test is the link and the beam geometry. Upgrading this row to Validated needs a measured transmit pattern and LuGRE normal points to check against; neither is in the repository and neither is invented here. { "0.30" } round { "2" }, a finding (stays MODELLED): a measured-input link path (real SP3 positions, per-block transmit power, tabulated transmit and receive patterns, acquisition and tracking hysteresis) was checked against Montenbruck et al. { "2023" } Table { "3" } for GENESIS at { "6000" } km (pre-registered 5b414f0a): zenith-antenna means agree on { "5" } of { "6" } within one dB of the paper sensitivity; nadir-antenna means are { "1.0" } to { "2.4" } satellites higher than printed, plausibly because the paper used azimuth-dependent GPS side-lobe patterns that are not published. The lunar-distance figures of this row still use the Airy stand-in. { "0.30" }+ D7, a finding (stays MODELLED): the restated quantity, the C/N0 DIFFERENCE between Block IIR/IIR-M satellites tracked at one epoch, predicted with the NAVCEN per-SVN measured L1 patterns (two-dimensional, yaw-steering frame) against the LuGRE flight C/N0 (pre-registered 768cb62b: { "99" } % visible, pair RMS <= { "3.0" } dB, median <= { "2.0" } dB, at least { "50" } records and { "30" } pairs) has too little data: the { "26" } qualifying transit and lunar-orbit epochs hold { "15" } tracked IIR/IIR-M records (mostly IIF and III are tracked) and one pair. All { "15" } are predicted visible and the single pair's residual is -0.06 dB; nothing is claimed
seg-bed2679f88e5 = 闭式恒等式与内部一致性；不声称有外部参考基准（oracle）。月缘半角对照asin(R_earth/r_orbit) 检查，几何对照它检查；本行所组合的自由空间损耗、DOP核和SGP4传播各自在自己的行中经外部核对，本行不借用它们的状态。为何为MODELLED，原因在于发射方向图：给定角度处的增益取自均匀照射圆形口径的Airy方向图，而真实的GPS L1天线是十二单元螺旋阵列，主瓣经过整形、峰值偏离视轴以使整个地球盘面功率均匀，其旁瓣也不是Airy形。已发布的实测方向图存在，但未纳入本库，因此排序和数量级是可信的，而任何单颗卫星的dB值则不然。本行的首个实现曾有一处值得记录的错误：Airy表达式仅在前向半球有效，其自变量按sin(theta) 变化，在theta接近{ "180" }度时会绕回并在口径背后返回满视轴增益——于是首份报告中最强的两条链路是天线背向月球的卫星，而它们恰是仅有的两条越过阈值的链路。现在在口径背后模型采用平坦的背瓣下限，作为一个界给出，而不是编造方向图形状。有意缺失、且各使预算偏乐观的内容：掠过月缘的射线没有电离层或对流层损耗，没有极化、指向或实现损耗，且采用无折射延伸的球形地球。月球位置是输入，而不是星历查询，因为被检验的量是链路和波束几何。若要把本行升级为经独立核对，需要实测的发射方向图和LuGRE的正常点来核对；二者都不在仓库中，也都不在此编造。{ "0.30" }第{ "2" }轮，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：一条以实测为输入的链路路径（真实SP3位置、逐Block发射功率、列表化的发射和接收方向图、捕获与跟踪滞回）对照Montenbruck等{ "2023" }表{ "3" }中{ "6000" } km处的GENESIS做了核对（预先登记5b414f0a）：天顶天线均值在{ "6" }项中有{ "5" }项与论文灵敏度相差在一dB之内；天底天线均值比印刷值高{ "1.0" }至{ "2.4" }颗卫星，原因很可能是该论文使用了未公布的随方位角变化的GPS旁瓣方向图。本行的月球距离数字仍使用Airy替代。{ "0.30" }+ D7，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：重述后的量，即同一历元所跟踪的Block IIR/IIR-M卫星之间的C/N0差，用NAVCEN逐SVN的实测L1方向图（二维，偏航转向坐标系）预测，对照LuGRE飞行C/N0（预先登记768cb62b：{ "99" } % 可见，成对RMS <= { "3.0" } dB，中位数<= { "2.0" } dB，至少{ "50" }条记录和{ "30" }个配对），数据太少：{ "26" }个合格的转移和月球轨道历元仅含{ "15" }条被跟踪的IIR/IIR-M记录（所跟踪的多为IIF和III）和一个配对。全部{ "15" }条均被预测为可见，该单一配对的残差为 -0.06 dB；不作任何声明

# Two internal oracles, no external dataset. ({ "1" }) Closed-form round trip: the minimum detectable bias is produced by inverting the non-central chi-square tail on the non-centrality (raim::pbias) and is verified by feeding the resulting non-centrality back through raim::noncentral_chi2_cdf at the monitor's own threshold, which must return P_md. ({ "2" }) Injection vs analysis: a bias is written into the RF estimate of one axis and cross_raim::run_cross_raim is re-run, so the statistic compared against the analytic non-centrality is the one the monitor computed, not a re-derivation. A seeded Monte-Carlo of the statistic itself is carried in the tests as a third check; it is deliberately NOT in the report, because a sampled estimate would be slower and not reproducible bit-for-bit. ExternalDataset is declined: the quantity is the detection power of THIS monitor at THIS scenario's sigma allocation, and the sigma magnitudes the MDB is scaled by are Modelled representative inputs, not measurements. ReferenceImpl was also considered and declined — the Monte-Carlo samples the same statistic the analysis describes, so it catches transcription and coefficient errors but is not an independent implementation of the monitor. { "0.30" } round { "2" } (pre-registered 0135a9e4), blocked (stays MODELLED): SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2/brentq reproduce the report threshold (within { "5.4e-12" }), per-axis MDB, power curve, crossing multiple and ramp time-to-detect (within { "5.3e-10" }) on five configurations; the injected-fault statistics have no independent oracle and the sigma magnitudes are Modelled inputs, so the scoping awaits an owner decision
seg-b6b8dec4dcff = 两个内部参考基准，没有外部数据集。（{ "1" }）闭式往返：最小可检测偏差由对非中心参数反演非中心卡方尾部（raim::pbias）得出，并通过把所得非中心参数在监测器自身阈值处送回raim::noncentral_chi2_cdf来验证，其结果必须返回P_md。（{ "2" }）注入与解析对比：把偏差写入某一轴的射频估计并重新运行cross_raim::run_cross_raim，因此与解析非中心参数相比较的统计量是监测器自身算出的，而不是重新推导的。统计量本身的带随机种子的蒙特卡罗作为第三项核对放在测试中；它被有意不放入报告（NOT），因为抽样估计会更慢，且不能逐位复现。ExternalDataset被拒绝：被量化的是此监测器在此场景的sigma分配下的检测能力，而MDB所依据的sigma量值是有代表性的模型输入，不是测量值。ReferenceImpl也曾被考虑并被拒绝：蒙特卡罗抽样的是解析所描述的同一统计量，所以能发现抄写和系数错误，但不是该监测器的独立实现。{ "0.30" }第{ "2" }轮（预先登记0135a9e4），受阻（仍为MODELLED）：SciPy { "1.18.1" }的chi2/ncx2/brentq在五种配置上复现了报告阈值（偏差在{ "5.4e-12" }以内）、各轴MDB、功率曲线、越过倍数和斜坡检测时间（偏差在{ "5.3e-10" }以内）；注入故障的统计量没有独立的参考基准，且sigma量值是模型输入，因此范围界定有待所有者决定

# Mixed, and separated rather than pooled. The PATTERN underneath is externally validated and keeps its own row (a scipy.special.j1 fixture to < { "0.05" } dB in tests/validate_p1_orbital_footprint.rs, plus here the published Airy half-power abscissa x = { "1.61634" } located by bisection rather than assumed). The GEOMETRY is checked against closed-form triangle trigonometry written as a different expression from the dot product under test. The CONTAINMENT of the real beam by the approximate cone is derived algebra (28.019/√η vs { "29.479" } degrees per λ/D), so the correction can only be non-positive for η ≤ { "0.9035" }. But the in-beam COUNTS themselves have no external oracle: no published table gives how many satellites of a Moonlight/LCNS-class shell hold a south-polar site inside a given dish's half-power beam, and the constellation is an illustrative public-source approximation, not a flown ephemeris. The measured disagreement at the documented working point — { "0" } links in beam under the real pattern against { "28" } under the approximation, −{ "2.33" } satellites per epoch, worst epoch { "3" }, on { "76" } evaluated links — is therefore a MODELLED finding about the approximation, pinned as a regression literal (the nearest row sits { "0.069" }° from either beam edge, four orders of magnitude above any last-digit disagreement), not an externally validated coverage number. Labelling the row ExternalDataset on the strength of the pattern's own external anchor would be borrowed validation, which is the move the matrix invariants exist to prevent. { "0.30" } round { "2" } (pre-registered d9755d28), blocked (stays MODELLED): SPICE geometry and SciPy Bessel functions reproduce the off-boresight angle (worst { "3.0e-5" } deg), Airy gain and in-beam flags on { "6 336" } rows of flown-orbiter states and the documented working point; but on flown geometry the flags split by satellite identity (the nearest link is { "1.66" } deg from the beam edge), the { "5" } deg visibility is checked as a flag, not a numeric elevation, the symmetric-approximation flag applies the 31000/G rule of thumb to a validated angle without validating the rule, and the implied efficiencies have no oracle; the scoping awaits an owner decision
seg-f7f79e2dd6c5 = 混合，且分开而不是合并。其下的方向图（PATTERN）经过外部核对，保有自己的一行（tests/validate_p1_orbital_footprint.rs中一个scipy.special.j1的固定数据，偏差小于{ "0.05" } dB，此外这里还通过二分法求得了已发表的Airy半功率横坐标x = { "1.61634" }，而不是假定）。几何（GEOMETRY）对照闭式的三角形三角学核对，该三角学写成与被测的点积不同的表达式。真实波束被近似锥体包容（CONTAINMENT）是推导出的代数：每 λ/D为28.019/√η 对{ "29.479" }度，因此对 η ≤ { "0.9035" }，修正只可能为非正。但波束内计数本身（COUNTS）没有外部参考基准：没有已发表的表格给出一个Moonlight/LCNS类壳层中有多少颗卫星会让某个南极站点落在某口天线的半功率波束内，而且星座是示意性的、取自公开来源的近似，不是实际飞行的星历。在文档记载的工作点上实测到的分歧（在真实方向图下波束内有{ "0" }条链路，在近似下为{ "28" }条，每历元 −{ "2.33" }颗卫星，最差历元为{ "3" }，评估了{ "76" }条链路），因此是关于该近似的MODELLED发现，被固定为回归字面值（最近的一行距任一波束边缘{ "0.069" }°，比任何末位数字的分歧高出四个数量级），不是经外部核对的覆盖数字。凭方向图自身的外部锚点就把这一行标为ExternalDataset，将是借用核对，而这正是矩阵不变量所要防止的做法。{ "0.30" }第{ "2" }轮（预先登记d9755d28），受阻（仍为MODELLED）：SPICE几何和SciPy的Bessel函数在{ "6 336" }行实际飞行轨道器状态和文档记载的工作点上复现了偏离视轴角（最差{ "3.0e-5" } deg）、Airy增益和波束内标志；但在实际飞行几何上，标志按卫星身份而分裂（最近的链路距波束边缘{ "1.66" } deg），{ "5" } deg的可见性是作为标志核对的，不是数值仰角，对称近似的标志把31000/G经验法则应用于一个经核对的角度，却没有核对该法则，而隐含的效率没有参考基准；范围界定有待所有者决定

# The engine's own sdr correlator (sdr::correlate / synth_if / CaCode) stepped forward on seeded synthetic IF at a calibrated C/N₀ — a separate code path reaching the same numbers by numerical correlation of sampled IQ rather than by an algebraic jitter expression, so it is a different route and not a restatement — plus standard tabulated modified-Bessel I₀ values for the cycle-slip term. Loop theory per Kaplan & Hegarty ch. { "8" } and Viterbi/Gardner for the slip time. The row stays ReferenceImpl/MODELLED: the cross-check lives in this same codebase, and the bessel_i0 check validates one special function rather than the tracking model, so promoting the row on that basis would be self-serving labelling. ExternalDataset would need a recorded raw-IF dataset with ground-truth C/N₀ and an annotated loss-of-lock instant (TEXBAT/OAKBAT class); none ships in this tree, and even with the IQ those datasets publish no per-epoch loop-state truth, so a declared loss-of-lock time could only be validated against some other receiver's lock detector, which is a modelled choice and not an oracle. The loop bandwidths, integration time, correlator spacing, pull-in ratio, dwells, jammer power and antenna gains are representative band figures, not a datasheet. { "0.30" } round { "2" }, GNSS-SDR { "0.0.19" } (GPL-3.0, run as a program) on independently generated IF (pre-registered 6ef7698c, amendments to the measurement method disclosed), a finding (stays MODELLED): closed-loop PLL and DLL jitter agree within { "8" } % at { "35" } to { "45" } dB-Hz, the { "10" } Hz/s dynamic stress within { "1" } % and the first-order code ramp lag within { "2" } %; at { "30" } dB-Hz the PLL jitter is { "21" } % higher and the DLL jitter { "32" } % lower, the measured 15-degree-rule thresholds sit { "1.5" } dB above the engine, and the second-order loop slips { "0.5" } to { "2" } decades more often than the first-order slip-time bound. { "0.30" } revision: the Costas slip time now uses the loop SNR rho/4 (half-cycle slips); the default jitter_table log10_mean_time_to_cycle_slip_s falls from { "11.58" } to { "2.10" } at the { "25.47" } dB-Hz threshold
seg-c3794c8d85c5 = 引擎自身的sdr相关器（sdr::correlate / synth_if / CaCode）在以校准的C/N₀ 生成的、带随机种子的合成IF上逐步向前运行：这是一条独立的代码路径，通过对采样IQ的数值相关而不是代数抖动表达式得到同样的数值，所以是另一条路径，而不是同一说法的复述；另外还有周跳项所用的标准列表修正Bessel I₀ 值。环路理论依Kaplan与Hegarty第{ "8" }章，周跳时间依Viterbi和Gardner。这一行仍为ReferenceImpl/MODELLED：交叉核对存在于同一代码库中，bessel_i0核对所核对的是一个特殊函数而不是跟踪模型，因此据此提升该行就是自我标榜的标注。ExternalDataset需要一份带有真值C/N₀ 和带注释的失锁时刻的原始IF录制数据集（TEXBAT/OAKBAT一类）；本代码树中没有随附这样的数据，而且即使有IQ，这些数据集也不公布逐历元的环路状态真值，因此声明的失锁时间只能对照另一台接收机的锁定检测器来核对，而那是模型选择，不是参考基准。环路带宽、积分时间、相关器间隔、牵入比、驻留、干扰源功率和天线增益是有代表性的频段数值，不是数据手册。{ "0.30" }第{ "2" }轮，GNSS-SDR { "0.0.19" }（GPL-3.0，作为程序运行）在独立生成的IF上（预先登记6ef7698c，对测量方法的修订已披露），一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：闭环PLL和DLL抖动在{ "35" }至{ "45" } dB-Hz上相符到{ "8" } % 以内，{ "10" } Hz/s的动态应力相符到{ "1" } % 以内，一阶码斜坡滞后相符到{ "2" } % 以内；在{ "30" } dB-Hz时，PLL抖动高出{ "21" } %，DLL抖动低{ "32" } %，实测的15度规则阈值比引擎高{ "1.5" } dB，而二阶环路的周跳比一阶周跳时间界限频繁{ "0.5" }至{ "2" }个数量级。{ "0.30" }修订：Costas周跳时间现使用环路SNR rho/4（半周跳）；默认的jitter_table log10_mean_time_to_cycle_slip_s在{ "25.47" } dB-Hz阈值处由{ "11.58" }降至{ "2.10" }

# An independent in-repo route. Every Jacobian row is re-derived by central finite difference of lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s evaluated from the state's own Earth-fixed and Moon-body-fixed coordinates rotated forward through the frame chain — a path that computes no derivative and shares no expression with the analytic partials — and the information matrices the two routes build agree to < { "1e-6" } of the largest diagonal. The linear-algebra kernel this composes (information_matrix, crlb, sym_eig) is separately externally anchored against numpy.linalg.eigh / numpy.linalg.inv in tests/fim_observability_reference.rs, and the delay observable carries lunar_vlbi's own delta-DOR far-field oracle; the row does not borrow either status. Closed-form identities checked here and labelled as such: σ² and 1/N scaling, and √(p/trace(M)) as an AM-HM lower bound the computed sigma cannot beat, which coincides with the equipartition value only on an isotropic geometry — which is why the measured ratio is exactly the anisotropy the assumption discarded. { "0.30" } external comparison (Library: NAIF SPICE CSPICE N0067 via spiceypy { "8.2.0" }, MIT; DE440, ITRF93 earth_latest_high_prec, MOON_PA), pre-registered ({ "50863551" }) together with an engine-side model-error sensitivity run: every Jacobian row is rebuilt by central differences of converged Newtonian light-time differences from SPICE geometry (the light-time equation is a short script over SPICE positions; SPICE carries the geometry), with the schedule rebuilt from SPICE elevations. The schedule, rank, defect, information spectrum, condition number, station sigmas, headline, trace bound, equipartition ratios, free-network null space, line-of-sight sweep and beacon declination agree within the pre-registered { "1" } % / { "0.1" } deg, which sits above the engine known analytic-Moon error ({ "3.2e-3" } on sigmas, measured before the run). NumPy { "2.3.5" } redoes the linear algebra on the committed engine Jacobian to { "2e-13" } (P2). Validated as a Cramér-Rao bound for the stated reduced parameter set on the stated illustrative inputs: the Moon-centre ephemeris, clocks, troposphere and Earth-orientation parameters are held fixed in both legs, so it is not a real-campaign accuracy
seg-b259d4d18f42 = 一条独立的仓库内路径。每一行雅可比都由lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s的中心有限差分重新推导，该函数在状态自身的地固和月固坐标上取值，并通过坐标系链向前旋转：这条路径不计算任何导数，也与解析偏导数没有共用的表达式；两条路径构建的信息矩阵相符，偏差小于最大对角元的{ "1e-6" }。它所组合的线性代数内核（information_matrix、crlb、sym_eig）另外已在tests/fim_observability_reference.rs中对照numpy.linalg.eigh / numpy.linalg.inv取得外部锚定，而时延观测量带有lunar_vlbi自身的delta-DOR远场参考基准；该行不借用这两者的状态。这里核对的闭式恒等式并如此标注：σ² 和1/N缩放，以及作为AM-HM下界的 √(p/trace(M))（算出的sigma不可能优于它），它仅在各向同性几何上与均分值重合：这正是实测比值恰好就是该假设所舍弃的各向异性的原因。{ "0.30" }外部比较（库：NAIF SPICE CSPICE N0067，经spiceypy { "8.2.0" }，MIT；DE440、ITRF93 earth_latest_high_prec、MOON_PA），连同引擎一侧的模型误差敏感性运行一起预先登记（{ "50863551" }）：每一行雅可比都由SPICE几何得出的、已收敛的牛顿光行时差的中心差分重建（光行时方程是基于SPICE位置的一小段脚本；SPICE提供几何），计划由SPICE仰角重建。计划、秩、亏损、信息谱、条件数、测站sigma、头条数值、迹界、均分比值、自由网零空间、视线扫掠和信标赤纬，在预先登记的{ "1" } % / { "0.1" } deg以内相符，该范围高于引擎已知的解析月球误差（sigma上为{ "3.2e-3" }，在运行之前测得）。NumPy { "2.3.5" }在已提交的引擎雅可比上重做线性代数，相符到{ "2e-13" }（P2）。经独立核对的是：在所述示意性输入上、对所述缩减参数集的Cramér-Rao界：月心星历、时钟、对流层和地球定向参数在两侧都保持固定，因此它不是真实测评活动的精度

# Closed-form surfaces (InternalConsistency): the bare-sphere dip, a crater in a plateau on the sphere with an observer on and off its centre, and an analytic cone against its own rasterisation. # Internal consistency: the brute-force elevation scan, the hand-computed and orbit::dop reductions, and the closed-form Walker and geosynchronous geometry. Two-body orbits with an optional secular J2, a spherical body with the local vertical along the radius, geometric visibility only (no signal power, satellite health, terrain or third-body perturbation), and the relative phase between systems taken from different reference epochs, so it is not a snapshot of any date. { "0.30" } review, a finding (stays MODELLED): the presets meet the published one-sided DOP-availability floors (BeiDou OS Performance Standard { "3.0" }, Galileo OS SDD) at { "100" } %, but with the engine's PDOP doubled in a scratch copy they still meet them (worst point { "99.36" } %), so the floors cannot detect even a { "100" } % error in the quantity this row computes; tightening them after the run would be a post-hoc tolerance. Promotion needs a two-sided comparison of DOP values on identical geometry with a pre-set tolerance. { "0.30" } round { "2" } (tests/coverage_dop_full_oracle.rs, pre-registered add671d6), a finding (stays MODELLED): Orekit { "12.2" } positions, elevation, azimuth and ground tracks with gnss_lib_py { "1.0.4" } GDOP/PDOP/HDOP/VDOP (common clock) and a numpy inverse (one clock per constellation) agree on every bar on the Galileo, GPS, BeiDou, Galileo+GPS (one clock each) and a 1584-satellite Earth design (DOP within { "2e-14" } relative, identical fix, availability and visibility counts). On the Moon and Mars designs the elements, positions, visibility and tracks agree, but DOP exceeds the pre-set { "1e-9" } relative bar in cells whose largest PDOP is { "1434" } or more (up to { "8e-6" }), and { "4" } cells differ by one fix epoch on numerically singular geometry (engine pivot { "1e-10" } against numpy rank), so the row is not validated. { "0.30" } revisions: the TDOP/GDOP reference clock is now the lowest-numbered constellation in view (constellation-multi-gnss-coverage global GDOP max { "2.2236" } -> { "2.1205" }, mean { "1.122" } -> { "1.131" }), and per-constellation visible counts are summed as integers
seg-2c0f28426ab9 = 内部自洽：暴力仰角扫描、手算和orbit::dop的归约，以及闭式的沃克和地球同步几何。二体轨道，可选长期J2，球形天体且局部垂线沿半径方向，仅几何可见性（不含信号功率、卫星健康状态、地形或第三体摄动），而且各系统之间的相对相位取自不同的参考历元，因此它不是任何日期的快照。{ "0.30" }审查，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：这些预设在{ "100" } % 时达到已发表的单侧DOP可用性下限（北斗OS Performance Standard { "3.0" }，Galileo OS SDD），但在一份临时副本中把引擎的PDOP加倍后它们仍然达到（最差点{ "99.36" } %），因此这些下限连本行所计算的量中{ "100" } % 的误差都无法检出；运行之后再收紧它们，就成了事后容差。晋升需要在相同几何上对DOP值作双侧比较，并事先设定容差。{ "0.30" }第{ "2" }轮（tests/coverage_dop_full_oracle.rs，预先登记add671d6），一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：Orekit { "12.2" }的位置、仰角、方位角和星下点轨迹，加上gnss_lib_py { "1.0.4" }的GDOP/PDOP/HDOP/VDOP（共用一个钟）以及一个numpy求逆（每个星座一个钟），在Galileo、GPS、北斗、Galileo+GPS（各一个钟）以及一个1584颗卫星的地球设计上，每一项判据都一致（DOP相对误差在{ "2e-14" }以内，定位解、可用性和可见数完全一致）。在月球和火星设计上，根数、位置、可见性和轨迹一致，但在最大PDOP为{ "1434" }或更大的单元中，DOP超出事先设定的{ "1e-9" }相对判据（最多{ "8e-6" }），并且有{ "4" }个单元在数值奇异的几何上相差一个定位历元（引擎主元{ "1e-10" }对numpy秩），因此本行没有经过独立核对。{ "0.30" }修订：TDOP/GDOP参考钟现为可见的编号最小的星座（constellation-multi-gnss-coverage全球GDOP最大值{ "2.2236" } -> { "2.1205" }，均值{ "1.122" } -> { "1.131" }），各星座的可见数按整数求和

# WHAT IS EXTERNALLY CHECKED, stated narrowly: our Prometheus text exposition for an 84-line synthetic stream (label values with quotes, backslashes, line feeds and non-ASCII text, { "64" } reason series plus an 'other' fold, gate and position gauges) is accepted by prometheus_client { "0.26.0" }'s text parser, and the metric families, types, help text, label sets and values it reads equal the registry's state exactly (values by f64 equality). Our OTLP/HTTP JSON for the same registry parses with protobuf { "7.36.2" } json_format.Parse(ignore_unknown_fields=False) into opentelemetry-proto { "1.45.1" }'s ExportMetricsServiceRequest, and every metric name, description, unit, kind, monotonicity, aggregation temporality, attribute, value, start time and time equals the registry's state exactly. Both parsers were shown to reject an unknown field, a mistyped value and a malformed line (scripts/gen_telemetry_formats_ref.py). Schema { "1.3" } ({ "0.36" }), pre-registered (fb226057) before any tool read it: for a 64-line synthetic schema-1.3 stream (geometry statuses, DOPs, labels and signal loss present, null, absent and unknown; deduction classes; unparsable lines) the same two parsers read the geometry series (Prometheus kshana_trust_geometry*, OTLP kshana.geometry.*) and the class label on kshana_trust_reason_active exactly as the registry implies ({ "24" } families and { "54" } samples; { "12" } metrics and { "31" } points), promtool { "3.5.0" } check metrics exits { "0" } with no lint problem, and the OpenTelemetry Collector contrib { "0.135.0" } accepts the OTLP/HTTP JSON (status { "200" }, nothing rejected) and its file exporter records the request unchanged (xval/telemetry-1.3, data-gated: the release tarballs are fetched with pinned SHA-256 and their outputs committed). WHAT IS NOT: the HTTP endpoint, delivery, timeouts and reconnection are not checked by an oracle, nor the Collector's prometheus receiver; the registry is read through accessors, so a fault in the registry state itself would not be caught; the OTLP 'committed output is current' check runs only with the otlp feature; the metric names and their usefulness to an operations centre are not validated; CEF and LEEF are not covered (see the next row).

# WHAT IS EXTERNALLY CHECKED, stated narrowly: against two independent open-source parsers (oracles: pycef { "1.11" } (PyPI, MIT); the Logstash CEF codec (logstash-codec-cef { "6.2.7" } in Logstash { "8.15.3" })), on a corpus of CEF lines this crate wrote ({ "157" } before amendment { "4" } below, { "170" } after it, { "176" } after amendment { "5" }): every band change and every epoch of a committed 84-line synthetic trust stream, plus adversarial text in hosts, reasons, time labels and gate names containing equals signs, backslashes, backslash-equals, pipes, line feeds, carriage returns, tabs, runs of spaces, multi-byte and astral characters and key lookalikes, extreme scores and offsets, every band with and without a previous band. pycef parses the lines whose header needs no escaping ({ "153" } before amendment { "4" }, { "166" } after it, { "172" } after amendment { "5" }) and recovers, exactly, the header fields, the severity, the exact extension key set and every value (numbers by f64 equality), after the CEF specification's unescape rules, which pycef does not apply and which scripts/gen_cef_ref.py applies. The Logstash codec, which unescapes by itself, parses every line ({ "157" }, then { "170" }, then { "176" }), meets the same bar, and also recovers four header fields that need escaping (a pipe, a backslash and an equals sign in the version string), which pycef's header pattern cannot read, so header escaping rests on the Logstash codec alone. The comparison bar was registered in commits { "5172e579" } (pycef) and 0a7598aa (Logstash) before either parser was run, and both met it at the first comparison. Five amendments are disclosed. Amendments ({ "1" }) to ({ "3" }) touched no comparison rule and no expected value: ({ "1" }) a corpus-size assertion was corrected from { "5" } to { "4" } band changes, a miscount, since the stream has { "4" }; ({ "2" }) the Logstash runner's pipeline delimiter wiring was fixed after a first attempt that produced no events; ({ "3" }) the trust score and epoch offset were moved from the CEF integer fields cn1 and cn2 to the floating-point fields cfp1 and cfp2, a wire-format change made in this release, after which the corpus was regenerated and both oracles were re-run with the bar unchanged and met it again. Amendment ({ "4" }), { "0.36" }, extended the corpus and the expected key set, registered in d6331429 before either parser ran on the new lines: schema { "1.3" } inputs add the satellite geometry in the custom fields cs6 (label geometry: status, then ';' and the deduction classes), cfp3 (geometryHdop) and cfp4 (geometryPdop); { "13" } geometry cases (the corpus to { "170" } lines) and the three new keys; both met the bar, otherwise unchanged, at the first comparison (pycef { "166" } of { "170" } lines parsed, the { "4" } tier { "2" } header cases as before; Logstash { "170" } of { "170" }). Amendment ({ "5" }), { "0.36" }, appended { "6" } geometry-label cases (the corpus to { "176" } lines), registered in 9a8e4fa9 before either parser ran on them: from inputs that carry the geometry label, cs6 gains a third field (';' and the label) and cn1 (geometryUsedSatellites) the satellites used; the Logstash codec's mapping table was read first (no flexString fields, hence cs6 rather than flexString1); both met the bar at the first comparison (pycef { "172" } of { "176" }, Logstash { "176" } of { "176" }), and every earlier reference entry was reproduced unchanged. WHAT IS NOT: neither parser type-checks the cfp values (both keep them as text), so the use of the floating-point fields rests on the CEF dictionary rather than on an oracle; no vendor SIEM was run; the extension key names and their mapping are this crate's choice, and the parsers show only that they are read back as written; the RFC { "5424" } envelope, delivery, timeouts and reconnection are not covered; pycef is a small parser last released in { "2020" }, and the Logstash codec is the more substantial oracle.

# <b>Impulsive transfer optimiser on DE440</b> ({ "0.36" }, WP-14, CAP-20c/d): <c1/> finds the departure and arrival dates of a single-revolution ballistic two-impulse transfer that minimise departure C3 or arrival V-infinity within Type I or Type II. The comparison was pre-registered (bf0439e8) against an independent SPICE + pykep { "3.0.1" } + SciPy { "1.18.1" } optimiser on the { "2026" } Earth-to-Mars opportunity: all four optimisations agree to { "8.7e-13" } relative, with dates within { "1e-5" } day (VALIDATED, new ledger row). seg-ce6d0ecab448 = 该服务器是一个<b>独立的、被排除在工作区之外的crate</b>，有自己的<c1/>，因此官方的Rust MCP SDK（软件开发工具包）<c2/>及其异步运行时绝不会进入已发布的<c3/> crate的依赖图：使用库或Python的用户不会去编译一个他们并不运行的服务器。（最初将它拆出，是因为<c4/>采用<b>edition { "2024" }</b>，需要rustc ≥ { "1.85" }，而主crate当时承诺的是{ "1.75" }。库现在声明{ "1.85" }；本服务器声明{ "1.88" }，即经过安全补丁的<c5/> { "2" }.x的下限。）持续集成（CI）单独对该crate设置门禁：针对其锁文件的测试和<c6/>。rmcp的许可证树是干净的（Apache-2.0/MIT/BSD），因此，与它在其他方面所仿照的<c7/>交叉验证crate不同，这次拆分与许可证无关。

# checked against its own closed-form / analytic identity — catches transcription and coefficient errors, but is not an external oracle — Closed-form identities and internal consistency; NO external oracle is claimed. The limb half angle is checked against asin(R_earth/r_orbit) and the geometry against it; the free-space loss, the DOP kernel and the SGP4 propagation this composes are each externally validated in their own rows and this row does not borrow their status. WHY MODELLED, and it is the transmit pattern: the gain at angle is a uniformly illuminated circular aperture, the Airy pattern, while a real GPS L1 antenna is a twelve-element helical array with a shaped main lobe peaked off-boresight to even out power across the Earth disc and sidelobes that are not Airy. Published measured patterns exist and are not vendored here, so orderings and orders of magnitude carry, while the dB of any single satellite does not. A FIRST IMPLEMENTATION OF THIS ROW WAS WRONG IN A WAY WORTH RECORDING: the Airy expression is valid only in the forward hemisphere, its argument goes as sin(theta), and at theta near { "180" } deg it wraps around and returns FULL BORESIGHT GAIN behind the aperture - so the two strongest links in the first report were satellites pointing their antennas away from the Moon, and they were the only two that cleared the threshold. Behind the aperture the model now applies a flat back-lobe floor, stated as a bound rather than a fabricated pattern shape. Deliberately absent, each making the budget OPTIMISTIC: no ionospheric or tropospheric loss on the limb-grazing rays, no polarisation, pointing or implementation loss, and a spherical Earth with no refractive extension. The Moon position is an INPUT, not an ephemeris lookup, because the quantity under test is the link and the beam geometry. Upgrading this row to Validated needs a measured transmit pattern and LuGRE normal points to check against; neither is in the repository and neither is invented here. { "0.30" } round { "2" }, a finding (stays MODELLED): a measured-input link path (real SP3 positions, per-block transmit power, tabulated transmit and receive patterns, acquisition and tracking hysteresis) was checked against Montenbruck et al. { "2023" } Table { "3" } for GENESIS at { "6000" } km (pre-registered 5b414f0a): zenith-antenna means agree on { "5" } of { "6" } within one dB of the paper sensitivity; nadir-antenna means are { "1.0" } to { "2.4" } satellites higher than printed, plausibly because the paper used azimuth-dependent GPS side-lobe patterns that are not published. The lunar-distance figures of this row still use the Airy stand-in. { "0.30" }+ D7, a finding (stays MODELLED): the restated quantity, the C/N0 DIFFERENCE between Block IIR/IIR-M satellites tracked at one epoch, predicted with the NAVCEN per-SVN measured L1 patterns (two-dimensional, yaw-steering frame) against the LuGRE flight C/N0 (pre-registered 768cb62b: { "99" } % visible, pair RMS <= { "3.0" } dB, median <= { "2.0" } dB, at least { "50" } records and { "30" } pairs) has too little data: the { "26" } qualifying transit and lunar-orbit epochs hold { "15" } tracked IIR/IIR-M records (mostly IIF and III are tracked) and one pair. All { "15" } are predicted visible and the single pair's residual is -0.06 dB; nothing is claimed
seg-ed40da9bdfae = 与其自身的闭式／解析恒等式核对——可发现转录和系数错误，但不是外部参考基准（oracle）——闭式恒等式与内部自洽；不声称有外部参考基准。临边半角对照asin(R_earth/r_orbit) 核对，几何也对照它核对；其所组合的自由空间损耗、DOP核和SGP4传播各自在自己的行中经过外部验证，本行不借用它们的状态。为何为MODELLED，原因在于发射方向图：随角度变化的增益取均匀照射的圆形口径，即Airy方向图，而真实的GPS L1天线是十二单元螺旋阵列，其赋形主瓣峰值偏离视轴，以使整个地球圆盘上的功率均匀，且旁瓣并非Airy型。已发布的实测方向图存在，但此处未收录，因此排序和数量级是可信的，而任何单颗卫星的dB值则不可信。本行的第一版实现有一处错误，值得记录：Airy表达式仅在前半球有效，其自变量按sin(theta) 变化，在theta接近{ "180" }度时会回绕，在口径背后返回满视轴增益——因此第一份报告中最强的两条链路，是天线背向月球的卫星，而它们恰好是仅有的两条越过门限的链路。在口径背后，模型现在采用平坦的后瓣底限，其表述为一个界，而不是杜撰的方向图形状。刻意缺失的内容，每一项都使预算偏乐观：临边掠过的射线没有电离层或对流层损耗，没有极化、指向或实现损耗，且地球为球形、没有折射延伸。月球位置是输入，不是星历查询，因为被检验的量是链路和波束几何。要将本行升级为已验证，需要实测发射方向图和LuGRE标准点用于核对；二者都不在仓库中，也都没有在此杜撰。{ "0.30" }第{ "2" }轮，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：一条以实测为输入的链路路径（真实SP3位置、逐Block发射功率、制表的发射和接收方向图、捕获与跟踪迟滞）已对照Montenbruck等{ "2023" }表{ "3" }中{ "6000" } km处的GENESIS核对（预先登记5b414f0a）：天顶天线均值在{ "6" }项中有{ "5" }项与论文灵敏度相差在一dB以内；天底天线均值比所印值高{ "1.0" }至{ "2.4" }颗卫星，可能是因为论文使用了随方位变化的GPS旁瓣方向图，而这些方向图并未发布。本行的月球距离数字仍使用Airy替代。{ "0.30" }+ D7，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：重述后的量，即同一历元所跟踪的Block IIR/IIR-M卫星之间的C/N0差，用NAVCEN逐SVN实测L1方向图（二维，偏航转向坐标系）预测，并对照LuGRE飞行C/N0（预先登记768cb62b：{ "99" } % 可见，成对RMS <= { "3.0" } dB，中位数<= { "2.0" } dB，至少{ "50" }条记录和{ "30" }对）——数据太少：{ "26" }个合格的过境和月球轨道历元中，只有{ "15" }条被跟踪的IIR/IIR-M记录（所跟踪的大多是IIF和III）和一对。全部{ "15" }条都被预测为可见，唯一一对的残差为 -0.06 dB；不作任何声明

# checked against its own closed-form / analytic identity — catches transcription and coefficient errors, but is not an external oracle — Two internal oracles, no external dataset. ({ "1" }) Closed-form round trip: the minimum detectable bias is produced by inverting the non-central chi-square tail on the non-centrality (raim::pbias) and is verified by feeding the resulting non-centrality back through raim::noncentral_chi2_cdf at the monitor's own threshold, which must return P_md. ({ "2" }) Injection vs analysis: a bias is written into the RF estimate of one axis and cross_raim::run_cross_raim is re-run, so the statistic compared against the analytic non-centrality is the one the monitor computed, not a re-derivation. A seeded Monte-Carlo of the statistic itself is carried in the tests as a third check; it is deliberately NOT in the report, because a sampled estimate would be slower and not reproducible bit-for-bit. ExternalDataset is declined: the quantity is the detection power of THIS monitor at THIS scenario's sigma allocation, and the sigma magnitudes the MDB is scaled by are Modelled representative inputs, not measurements. ReferenceImpl was also considered and declined — the Monte-Carlo samples the same statistic the analysis describes, so it catches transcription and coefficient errors but is not an independent implementation of the monitor. { "0.30" } round { "2" } (pre-registered 0135a9e4), blocked (stays MODELLED): SciPy { "1.18.1" } chi2/ncx2/brentq reproduce the report threshold (within { "5.4e-12" }), per-axis MDB, power curve, crossing multiple and ramp time-to-detect (within { "5.3e-10" }) on five configurations; the injected-fault statistics have no independent oracle and the sigma magnitudes are Modelled inputs, so the scoping awaits an owner decision

# checked against a separate implementation in this same codebase — independent of the unit under test, but not externally authoritative — Closed-form identities and an independent in-repo route, with no external oracle claimed. The Helmert design — the only new derivative in the module — is re-derived by central finite difference of lunar_frame_realise::apply_helmert, a path sharing no expression with the analytic form. The accumulation it composes is lunar-vlbi-fim's, whose Jacobian is finite-differenced against lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s there, and whose linear-algebra kernel is separately externally anchored against numpy in tests/fim_observability_reference.rs — this row borrows neither status. The physics oracle is structural rather than numerical: a beacon delay partial is the near-field DIFFERENCE of two near-parallel unit vectors, so the worst-determined translation direction MUST be the body-fixed direction to Earth, and the test asserts the computed answer against the separately measured direction. MEASURED: campaign-derived translation sigma { "6.3975" } m against the injected-transform scenario's { "0.3125" } m recovery error ({ "20.5" }× tighter), while rotation and scale run the OTHER way at { "2.32" }× and { "13.5" }× looser — the injected path assumes one isotropic sigma and so spreads its error uniformly across seven parameters, which the delay observable does not. Rank 7/7 but condition { "3.8e5" }, with one direction { "99.57" } % pure translation-toward-Earth and forty times worse than any other. WHAT THIS ROW DOES NOT CLAIM: that the campaign figure is right in absolute terms. It establishes that the figure now DERIVES from a schedule, a geometry and an error model rather than from a planted answer. Stays ReferenceImpl/MODELLED: no published lunar-VLBI campaign-plus-datum-covariance pair exists, the station network and delay sigma are ILLUSTRATIVE inputs (the beacon catalogue is sourced, the campaign is not), observations are treated as independent while a real session's troposphere and clock are correlated between nearby scans, and the ephemeris, clocks, troposphere and Earth-orientation parameters are held FIXED. { "0.30" } external comparison, a finding (stays MODELLED): with every beacon Jacobian row rebuilt from NAIF SPICE light times (DE440, ITRF93, MOON_ME; pre-registered { "50863551" }), the schedule, Helmert rank, datum sigmas (within { "4.7e-3" }), condition, weakest direction ({ "0.07" } deg), libration sweep and arc ordering agree on six scenarios within the pre-registered { "1" } % / { "0.2" } / { "0.05" } deg. NumPy on the committed engine Jacobian agrees to { "3e-14" } where the stations are fixed. With the stations estimated it differs by { "6.8e-6" } against a pre-registered { "1e-9" }, whose condition-number premise was wrong (Helmert condition { "2.1e8" }; independent NumPy routes disagree by { "3e-6" }; a contribution from the engine { "1e-9" } pseudo-inverse fallback is not ruled out), so the row is not promoted on that pre-registration. { "0.36" } re-registration at a condition-scaled bar (pre-registered b887eb05; t = max({ "1e-12" }, { "10" } kappa { "2" }^-53) from the conditioning of the joint information and its Helmert reduction), a finding (stays MODELLED, NOT MET): step { "0" } rules the pseudo-inverse fallback OUT for the { "6.8e-6" } gap (no matrix upstream of the Helmert matrix falls back on the six original scenarios, tightest margin a factor { "2.6" }), the SPICE leg still holds on all six, and NumPy agrees within the bar on six of eight scenarios (default, the two arcs, the 3x delay sigma, the collinear defect and the fresh stations-fixed run on 2026-12-01; worst { "0.13" } of its bar). seg-546d8034c488 = 与本代码库内另一独立实现核对——独立于被测单元，但不具外部权威性——闭式恒等式和一条仓库内的独立路径，没有声称外部参考基准（oracle）。Helmert设计——该模块中唯一新增的导数——通过对lunar_frame_realise::apply_helmert做中心有限差分重新推导，这条路径与解析形式不共享任何表达式。它所组合的累积是lunar-vlbi-fim的，其雅可比矩阵已在那里对照lunar_vlbi::vlbi_delay_s做了有限差分，其线性代数核函数则在tests/fim_observability_reference.rs中另行对照numpy做了外部锚定——本行不借用其中任何一种地位。物理参考基准（oracle）是结构性的而不是数值性的：信标时延偏导是两个近平行单位向量的近场差，因此被确定得最差的平移方向必须是指向地球的体固方向，测试把计算出的答案对照另行MEASURED的方向来断言。实测：由测评活动推导的平移sigma为{ "6.3975" } m，而注入变换场景的恢复误差为{ "0.3125" } m（前者收紧{ "20.5" }×），而旋转和尺度则相反，分别宽松{ "2.32" }× 和{ "13.5" }×——注入路径假定一个各向同性sigma，因此把其误差均匀分摊到七个参数上，而时延观测量并非如此。秩为7/7，但条件数为{ "3.8e5" }，其中一个方向有{ "99.57" } % 是纯粹指向地球的平移，比其他任何方向差四十倍。本行不主张的内容：测评活动的数字在绝对意义上是正确的。它确立的是：该数字现在由时间表、几何和误差模型推导得出，而不是来自预先植入的答案。仍为ReferenceImpl/MODELLED：不存在已发表的月球VLBI测评活动加基准协方差的配对，站点网络和时延sigma是ILLUSTRATIVE输入（信标星表有来源，测评活动则没有），观测被视为相互独立，而真实时段中邻近扫描之间的对流层和时钟是相关的，星历、时钟、对流层和地球定向参数均保持固定。{ "0.30" }外部比较，一项发现（仍为MODELLED）：在每一个信标雅可比行都由NAIF SPICE光行时重建的情况下（DE440、ITRF93、MOON_ME；预先登记{ "50863551" }），时间表、Helmert秩、基准sigma（在{ "4.7e-3" }之内）、条件数、最弱方向（{ "0.07" }度）、天平动扫描和弧段排序在六个场景上相符，处于预先登记的{ "1" } % / { "0.2" } / { "0.05" }度之内。在已提交的引擎雅可比矩阵上，NumPy在站点固定时相符至{ "3e-14" }。在站点被估计时，二者相差{ "6.8e-6" }，而预先登记的为{ "1e-9" }，其条件数前提是错误的（Helmert条件数{ "2.1e8" }；独立的NumPy路径彼此相差{ "3e-6" }；引擎{ "1e-9" }伪逆回退的贡献未被排除），因此该行不据此预先登记而提升等级。{ "0.36" }在按条件数缩放的判据下重新登记（预先登记b887eb05；t = max({ "1e-12" }, { "10" } kappa { "2" }^-53)，来自联合信息及其Helmert约化的条件数），一项发现（仍为MODELLED，NOT MET）：第{ "0" }步排除了伪逆回退是造成{ "6.8e-6" }差距的原因（在六个原始场景中，Helmert矩阵上游没有任何矩阵发生回退，最紧的裕度为{ "2.6" }倍），SPICE环节在全部六个场景上仍然成立，NumPy在八个场景中的六个上相符于判据之内（默认、两种弧段、3x时延sigma、共线缺陷，以及2026-12-01的全新站点固定运行；最差为其判据的{ "0.13" }）。它在stations_estimated上未通过（基准sigma最高{ "6.8e-6" }，条件数{ "9.1e-6" }，舍弃独立性的范数{ "3.5e-6" }，对照t = { "5.9e-7" }），而在全新日期上南极信标没有观测，因此信标信息秩亏（两个全新场景中Helmert矩阵上游均发生伪逆回退），未改动的NumPy参考基准（oracle）无法评估stations_estimated_fresh（奇异矩阵）。结果：docs/design/evidence/mr05-lunar-datum-cond/RESULTS.md

# <b>What the observatory does instead.</b> The question the almanac comparison answers is "could the constellation geometry alone explain this low accuracy?". The base method answers a related question without an almanac: a low share must stand out against the same day's background across the region and against the aircraft's own good reports elsewhere. That is a different and weaker substitute: it cannot say that a quiet-geometry hour was degraded where the day's background is also low. An almanac-HDOP scorer is <b>not implemented</b>. It is an open item, to be built from the paper's full text and the public GPS almanac and pre-registered before it is run; until then it is cited, not claimed.
seg-4f8471649a07 = <b>观测站转而做什么。</b>星历比较所回答的问题是“仅凭星座几何能否解释这一低精度？”。基础方法在不使用星历的情况下回答一个相关的问题：较低的比例必须在该地区同一天的背景中，以及在该飞机自己在别处的良好报告面前，显得突出。这是一种不同的、较弱的替代：在当天背景同样较低之处，它无法说明某个几何条件平静的小时是否曾降级。星历HDOP评分器<b>尚未实现</b>。这是一个未决事项，需依据该论文的全文和公开的GPS星历来构建，并在运行之前预先登记；在此之前，它只是被引用，而不是被声称。
